**[2760.00s] English:** PCC is like the Olympics of grappling.  
**Translation:** 

**[2762.18s] English:** It occurs every two years.  
**Translation:** 

**[2764.88s] English:** You can either qualify for the event through winning matches and a qualification process, or you can be invited.  
**Translation:** Vocabulary: qualification: 选拔过程

**[2772.80s] English:** The only people who get invited are either former winners or people in the sport who are just widely recognized superstars who bring some kind of brand value, who have proven in the past that they have what it takes to compete at that level.  
**Translation:** 

**[2785.92s] English:** Now, in this format, there are two kinds of matches.  
**Translation:** 

**[2792.42s] English:** There are weight division matches in which you compete against people roughly your own size and weight.  
**Translation:** 

**[2805.18s] English:** There is an open weight where anyone of any size can enter, so you can have very small people fighting very large people.  
**Translation:** 

**[2813.56s] English:** And there is a second category.  
**Translation:** 

**[2815.92s] English:** It's called a super fight, where established champions who have won previous open weight tournaments fight each other in one-off battles, one athlete against another.  
**Translation:** 

**[2829.44s] English:** So in most of the matches, you will fight repetitively over time towards a gold medal.  
**Translation:** 

**[2835.78s] English:** But in one category, you fight one fight, the so-called super fight, which is usually the headline fight of the event.  
**Translation:** Vocabulary: repetitively: 重复地

**[2843.98s] English:** Traditionally, if you were in the super fight, you would fight one fight.  
**Translation:** 

**[2845.92s] English:** If you were in the super fight, you could not compete in the weight categories.  
**Translation:** 

**[2849.82s] English:** It was seen as too risky because you might get injured during the weight category, or you might have to fight four very tough fights in a row and get exhausted so that you're ineffective during the main event of the show, the super fight.  
**Translation:** 

**[2865.58s] English:** So throughout its history, ADCC has always resisted the idea of an athlete being allowed to do both weight category and super fight.  
**Translation:** 

**[2874.16s] English:** It's never happened before.  
**Translation:** 

**[2875.92s] English:** Gordon Ryan requested to be able to do this.  
**Translation:** 

**[2880.00s] English:** Because of his extraordinary stature in the sport, the ADCC organization granted his request.  
**Translation:** 

**[2886.42s] English:** That was the first time ever.  
**Translation:** 

**[2888.80s] English:** In addition, Gordon Ryan would be fighting to be the first person to win three gold medals in three different weight categories.  
**Translation:** 

**[2896.54s] English:** This had never been done before.  
**Translation:** 

**[2899.18s] English:** So it was a huge event on Gordon's part.  
**Translation:** 

**[2902.18s] English:** And bear in mind also that prior to this event, he had fought just a month and a half earlier against a former ADCC openweight champion, Felipe Pena, who had defeated him in the past in a completely different rule set.  
**Translation:** Vocabulary: felipe: 费利佩; openweight: 轻重量级

**[2915.64s] English:** And then previous to that against the current world champion.  
**Translation:** 

**[2918.44s] English:** So there had been a build up to this.  
**Translation:** 

**[2920.32s] English:** So he had been very active coming up to the event.  
**Translation:** 

**[2925.64s] English:** And then he went in to fight arguably the greatest ADCC champion of all time, Andre Galvan, which would occur later.  
**Translation:** Vocabulary: andre: 安德烈; arguably: 可以说; galvan: 加尔万

**[2932.18s] English:** And would have to fight the toughest people, including the possibility of fighting his nemesis, Felipe Pena, in the weight division prior to getting to the super fight.  
**Translation:** 

**[2946.38s] English:** So there was genuine concern here that he may have completely overstepped himself.  
**Translation:** Vocabulary: nemesis: 宿敌; overstepped: 逾矩; toughest: 最强的

**[2951.94s] English:** The biggest concern I had as a coach, and I'm sure the organizers, Mo Jasim, must have had the same concern, is that he would get injured or exhausted fighting in his weight division.  
**Translation:** 

**[2962.18s] English:** There were two athletes in particular, Felipe Pena, who had given Gordon a very tough 40-minute match in a no-rules setting shortly before ADCC, and his former training partner, Nick Rodriguez, who were expected to give Gordon very, very tough matches if they came up against each other.  
**Translation:** Vocabulary: organizers: 主办方; rodriguez: 罗德里格斯

**[2986.70s] English:** So there was a genuine concern that Gordon may burn himself out before he even got to fight.  
**Translation:** 

**[2992.18s] English:** The guy who most people believe is the greatest ADCC champion of all time.  
**Translation:** 

**[2996.48s] English:** So our concern was, how do we manage this?  
**Translation:** 

**[3000.00s] English:** So what we looked for is extremely efficient methods of reducing the time of the matches, making the matches as short as possible.

**[3010.44s] English:** Our favorite way to fight bigger, stronger athletes, and I think Gordon was the lightest athlete in his weight division.
